Ash: Make the DLP warning notifier own its dialog widgets

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The DLP warning notifier now owns each warning dialog widget it shows
instead of tracking raw pointers to them, and destroys a dialog as soon
as it closes or its window goes away.

[Extensions] Start ExtensionUpdater before CheckManagementPolicy.

During profile startup, ExtensionService::Init() loads saved extensions
and calls CheckManagementPolicy() before OnInstalledExtensionsLoaded().
Because ExtensionUpdater::InitAndEnable() has already run in the
ExtensionService constructor, CheckManagementPolicy() sees
updater_->enabled() as true and calls updater_->CheckNow() for any
installed extension disabled solely due to failing the enterprise
minimum_version_required policy. Since updater_->Start() was not called
until OnInstalledExtensionsLoaded(), CheckNow() crashes on
CHECK(alive_).

Start updater_ in ExtensionService::Init() before calling
CheckManagementPolicy() instead of inside OnInstalledExtensionsLoaded().
This ensures ExtensionUpdater is alive when CheckManagementPolicy()
triggers an update check at startup, while keeping the remaining
OnInstalledExtensionsLoaded() work (including startup DSE/NTP override
UMA metrics) after policy evaluation and avoiding repeated Start() calls
in ReloadExtensionsForTest().

TAG=agy
CONV=0a20d0a6-b6ab-4fec-b70e-b5d1714e58a6

Ash: Make tray bubbles own their widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The object that shows a status area tray bubble now owns the bubble's
widget. When the bubble closes, its tray keeps the closed widget and
frees it in a later task, once its window is gone.

Ash: Move the Magic Boost opt-in card to a client-owned widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The Magic Boost card controller now frees the opt-in card widget a
moment after its window goes away. Closing the card still hides its
window at once.

[3p freshness] expat: Roll to R_2_9_0 and roll by release tags

Roll src/third_party/expat/src from a851869e111c (R_2_8_1+189,
2026-06-22) to a76b1174c1ea (R_2_9_0, 2026-10-05), 369 commits. The
previous pin had missed five upstream releases (R_2_8_2 to R_2_9_0).

Expat is now rolled to upstream release tags instead of upstream/master,
in preparation for a Skia Autoroller that rolls by tag:
- roll-expat.sh rolls to the newest upstream/R_X_Y_Z tag and sets
  Version to the tag name (e.g. R_2_9_0) and the CPEPrefix version from
  it.
- README.chromium: Version, CPEPrefix, Date and Revision for R_2_9_0,
  and the roll instructions.

Upstream changes that need Chromium changes:
- New source file lib/xcs.c (xmlparse.c now calls xcslen()), and
  lib/xcsinc.c became lib/xcs.h: added to BUILD.gn.
- expat_config.h: version 2.9.0, and XML_ATTR_INFO is gone from
  configure.
- Expat 2.8.5 rejects an XML declaration version other than 1.x itself,
  with XML_ERROR_XML_DECL (upstream #1354), which changes the error
  libjingle_xmpp's XmlParserTest.TestError sees. That test is updated
  first in https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8814581.

expat_config.h keeps XML_DEV_URANDOM, which the checked-in file has
always defined but the documented ./configure flags don't produce
(configure only defines it with --with-dev-urandom). The flag is now
added to roll-expat.sh and README.chromium so that regenerating the file
keeps it. With the flag, ./configure output differs from the checked-in
file only in the changes above (and autoconf comment wording, which is
left as is).

On Linux and ChromeOS, Chromium uses the system expat; the in-tree
sources are built on Android, Windows, Mac and fuzzer builds. Tested:
gn check (Linux, Android, ChromeOS), Android build of
//third_party/expat:expat, Linux chrome and expat_xml_parse_fuzzer, and
libjingle_xmpp_unittests with the in-tree Expat (Linux libFuzzer/ASan
build).

0a87361b9e48bb30c4e4ed8c23f5aa1f77038a2a [ diff ] 
blink: Replace <cstdint> with <stdint.h> where needed

The Google C++ Style Guide states that fixed-width integer types should
not use `std::`, and `<cstdint>` is not guaranteed to provide them
without `std::` (unlike `<stdint.h>`).

Remove `#include <cstdint>` in `third_party/blink/renderer/platform/`,
include `<stdint.h>` where needed, and drop remaining `std::` prefixes.

This CL has no behavior changes.

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted

fragment (and, for the session status, its error-document flag), parse
it and record the histogram synchronously, so the histogram is already
recorded when they return. The calls only drained the DidStopLoading()
notification that TestAshWebView::Navigate() posts, which no observer
in this fixture listens to.

Ash: Migrate ScreensaverImageDownloaderTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

The tests now wait with RunUntil() for the in-flight URL request
(TestURLLoaderFactory::IsPending()) where a download is expected to be
waiting for its response, and for the kFileSaveError sample that the
last of the serialized error downloads records. The folder deletion
check waits for a reply posted to the downloader's own sequenced task
runner after the delete, and ClearImagesAfterUpdateTest waits for the
second image-list callback from the cached image. Three calls are
deleted: QueueImageDownload() only appends to the queue while a
download runs, and FinishImageDownload(), which ran the awaited
callback, posts nothing once the queue is empty. The call in
ClearRequestQueueWhenEmptyListIsPassedTest stays: it checks that the
cancelled download writes no file, and its response is dropped through
an invalidated WeakPtr with no observable event to wait for.

Ash: Migrate AmbientWeatherControllerTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

RefreshesWeather now waits for AmbientWeatherModelObserver's
OnWeatherInfoUpdated(). CreateScopedRefresher() requests the weather
synchronously and the fake backend answers inline, but
TestImageDownloader posts the condition icon reply, and the model
updates only when it runs. RunUntil() won't do here: under MOCK_TIME
it first fast-forwards to the 5 minute refresh timer, which fetches the
weather again. The other two calls are deleted. In
RespectsSystemLocationPermission, SetGeolocationAccessLevel()
synchronously clears the model through OnPermissionChanged(), and
CreateScopedRefresher() then fetches nothing. In DisabledByPolicy,
CreateScopedRefresher() skips FetchWeather(), which would have counted
the request synchronously.

Ash: Migrate ScreensaverImagesPolicyHandlerForAnySessionTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

In VerifyUnsetImageListPrefBehavior, the first two cases need no wait:
with the image list pref unset and the policy not disabled, the
handler's constructor returns without calling UpdateImageUrlList(), the
only path that deletes cache files, so nothing can remove them before
the checks. The third case keeps its RunUntilIdle(): the cache is
deleted by a task on the downloader's private thread pool runner with
no reply or callback, so the test has nothing to wait on.

ab364755d6d888eb7ccdcf61ebca9d0bcde2060b [ diff ] 
blink: Remove <cstdint> includes in renderer/modules/

The Google C++ Style Guide states that fixed-width integer types should
not use `std::`, and `<cstdint>` is not guaranteed to provide them
without `std::` (unlike `<stdint.h>`).

Remove `#include <cstdint>` in `third_party/blink/renderer/platform/`,
include `<stdint.h>` where needed, and drop remaining `std::` prefixes.

This CL has no behavior changes.

parent
79b56dfb611c67522cc03bd6dbe2cba61e98e053 [ diff ] 
media: Validate H.265 alpha SPS and disable HEVC alpha in WebCodecs

In H265Decoder, auxiliary alpha-layer (nuh_layer_id ==
aux_alpha_layer_id_) SPS, PPS, and slice NALUs were previously parsed
and forwarded directly to H265Accelerator without checking that the
alpha layer's SPS geometry and format match the base layer. On macOS
Apple Silicon (VideoToolbox / AppleVideoDecoder), the output IOSurface
is sized from the base-layer CMVideoFormatDescription while hardware
alpha decoding writes to plane 2 using the layer-1 SPS dimensions,
causing an out-of-bounds IOSurface write when the alpha SPS declares
larger dimensions than the base SPS.

1. Plumb VideoDecoderConfig::AlphaMode into VideoToolboxH265Accelerator
   so IsAlphaLayerSupported() is only true when the config explicitly
   requests kHasAlpha (which disables H.265 alpha decoding in WebCodecs,
   where AlphaMode is always kIsOpaque, while preserving it for
   <video>).
2. Validate that every alpha-layer SPS, PPS, and slice matches the
   base-layer SPS in VPS ID, coded width, coded height, CTB geometry,
   chroma format, and bit depth before forwarding to the accelerator,
   plus defense-in-depth validation in
   VideoToolboxH265Accelerator::SubmitSlice().

Verification:
- Repro (Before): out/Linux/media_unittests
  --gtest_filter="H265DecoderTest.AlphaLayerGeometryMismatch*" -> FAILED
  (returned kRanOutOfStreamData instead of kDecodeError)
- Fix (After): out/Linux/media_unittests
  --gtest_filter="H265DecoderTest.AlphaLayer*" -> 6/6 tests passed
- Regression: out/Linux/media_unittests --gtest_filter="*H265*" -> 83/83
  tests passed, 0 failures

TAG=agy
CONV=2d4c460a-3e69-4469-8af4-678b9b258d30

Fix focus jumping to Tab Search when opening Site Settings on Linux
📋 Metadata

79b56dfb611c67522cc03bd6dbe2cba61e98e053
[ log ]
author

Tue Nov 17 07:39:16 2026
tree
f6d62e4562eeabe54aa06eef6272b70f6f1acbe0
parent
2f252a2f561a555039f0ab5f4641dc0f9cf706db [ diff ] 
Fix focus jumping to Tab Search when opening Site Settings on Linux

Selecting "Site settings" from the Page Info bubble opens a new
foreground Settings tab while the bubble widget still holds activation.
The bubble then only closes in the middle of
BrowserView::OnActiveTabChanged(), when the previous WebContents is
hidden. On Linux (X11 child widgets and Wayland bubbles), hiding the
active bubble synchronously re-activates the browser widget during the
tab switch, so focus is restored to the detached contents view and then
advanced to the first focusable view in Top Chrome (Tab Search), and
the new tab never gets focus.

Close the Page Info bubble before opening Site Settings, as
ContentSettingBubbleContents already does for its Settings links. This
returns activation to the browser window before the new tab is
activated, so the normal active-window path focuses the new tab.

Fixed: 515294767

6d2f78096e33a449fda748f85934a2ae84596029

author
Tove Petersson <tovep@google.com>
Tue Nov 17 07:30:40 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 17 07:30:40 2026
tree
c91b3f82533d73f6e837fec96b085443deb332a3
parent
983b8c20ddfb3f8bc9d4de7306caec4b2672e92d [ diff ] 
[views] Clear ViewGuard::view_ when NotFatalUntil is non-fatal

When ViewGuard is constructed with a base::NotFatalUntil milestone and
the guarded View is deleted while NotFatalUntil is non-fatal (such as in
Official builds prior to M165, or under
ScopedNotFatalUntilSeverityOverride in tests), OnViewIsDeleting() resets
observation_ and logs NOTREACHED, but previously left raw_ref<View>
view_ pointing to the freed View. When ~ViewGuard() subsequently runs at
scope exit, PartitionAlloc's DanglingPtrDetector reports a dangling
pointer.

Store view_ as raw_ptr<View> and clear it in OnViewIsDeleting() on the
non-fatal path.

Clear stale local-only formats in Android clipboard

Formats without an Android clipboard representation (such as SVG and
source URL) are cached only locally in native memory and persisted
across external clipboard changes. Clear these local-only entries on
clipboard change and window focus regain when the Android clip has a
newer timestamp than the last native write.

Because native image writes land asynchronously with a newer timestamp
than the initial commit, advance the recorded write time once the image
clip is written so it is not treated as an external change.

Use AppServiceRegistry in DesksClient::GetTemplateJson

DesksClient::GetTemplateJson only uses AppRegistryCache of
AppServiceProxy. Because DesksClient is a global singleton, which
tracks the active user at runtime, this CL switches it to look up
apps::AppService via apps::AppServiceRegistry with the AccountId of
the active user, which DesksClient already tracks, instead of
AppServiceProxyFactory.

The active user is always a regular user (see IsSupportedProfile()),
so AppService must exist for it.

tree
7cd61d8e8828e85c35ddedff0cf2f1c75fd4dc6a
parent
8ec31aa680bf1778801be01f5d6e265ae6ae940e [ diff ] 
[sync] Check FakeServer progress markers in UpdatedProgressMarkerChecker

UpdatedProgressMarkerChecker waits until all local changes have been
committed to the server and a subsequent GetUpdates cycle (triggered via
test-only self-notifications) has updated the client's progress markers.

Previously, it relied on a fragile heuristic: checking that
download_progress_markers() was non-empty and that the latest sync cycle
committed 0 items (num_successful_commits == 0), while
OnSyncCycleCompleted() overwrote has_unsynced_items_ with
snap.has_remaining_local_changes().

This heuristic had a race condition that surfaces when adopting a more
realistic asynchronous invalidation delivery mechanism in SyncTest (such
as FakeGCMServer):
- A sync cycle commits an entity (num_successful_commits > 0) and
  triggers an asynchronous self-invalidation.
- Before that self-invalidation arrives at the client, a spurious local
  nudge triggers a commit-only sync cycle that commits 0 items
  (num_successful_commits == 0).
- UpdatedProgressMarkerChecker observes non-empty progress markers from
  earlier cycles and num_successful_commits == 0 from the commit-only
  cycle, and exits prematurely before the self-invalidation GetUpdates
  cycle has run.
- In addition, setting has_unsynced_items_ from
  snap.has_remaining_local_changes() instead of re-querying the sync
  thread via HasUnsyncedItemsForTest() could miss local changes posted
  while a sync cycle was in flight.

This patch addresses the issue by:
- Passing FakeServer* explicitly to UpdatedProgressMarkerChecker and
  QuiesceStatusChangeChecker.
- Adding FakeServer::AreProgressMarkersUpToDate(), backed by
  LoopbackServer::AreProgressMarkersUpToDateForTesting() and
  UpdateSieve, to directly verify that all non-commit-only client
  progress markers are non-empty and up to date with all entities and
  migration versions on FakeServer.
- Re-querying HasUnsyncedItemsForTest() on the sync thread in
  OnSyncCycleCompleted(), matching CommittedAllNudgedChangesChecker, and
  evaluating has_unsynced_items_ first.

Alternatives considered (smaller test diffs by avoiding passing
FakeServer* to callers, at the cost of adding test-motivated state to
production SyncCycleContext/SyncCycleSnapshot instead of checking
server-side ground truth):
- Tracking max entity versions observed in GetUpdates and Commit
  responses on SyncCycleSnapshot (https://crrev.com/c/8815421).
- Tracking a cross-cycle GetUpdates counter since the last commit on
  SyncCycleSnapshot (https://crrev.com/c/8816335).
- Making ModelNeutralState::last_download_updates_result optional to
  require GetUpdates in the last cycle (https://crrev.com/c/8815199),
  which remains fragile because it only inspects the most recent cycle.

8f9e7c38175afc25c738ff5283bb5fe77ef31e0b
parent
e4e801edd515dc3197334726ba27f575bc17ed0e [ diff ] 
[Workers] Freeze Worklets and OfflineAudioContext in BFCache

Worklet only observed ExecutionContext destruction and did not forward FrameLifecycleState::kFrozen or kRunning to its WorkletGlobalScopeProxy instances. As a result, threaded worklets kept their WorkerScheduler active and their ResourceFetchers unfrozen when the owning document entered the Back-Forward Cache, allowing in-flight addModule() calls and MessagePort tasks to continue fetching and executing script while cached. OfflineAudioContext also ignored lifecycle state changes, allowing an active offline render loop to continue invoking AudioWorkletProcessor.process() while frozen.

Make Worklet an ExecutionContextLifecycleStateObserver and forward Freeze() and Resume() through WorkletGlobalScopeProxy to main-thread and threaded WorkletGlobalScopes, pausing the worklet's WorkerScheduler, deferring its ResourceFetchers, and wiring BFCache loader buffer tracking and eviction. Also pause and resume OfflineAudioDestinationHandler when the execution context transitions between kFrozen and kRunning.

SECURITY_PRESUBMIT_BYPASS_REASON=AskedToTerminate() is checked before posting to BackingThread().GetTaskRunner(), and worker_thread_ is only destroyed in WorkerThreadTerminated() after PerformShutdownOnWorkerThread() runs on that same FIFO task runner.

> [Glic DLP] Scan extracted PDF bytes with FILE_ATTACHED connector

> dialog was showing, still complete the request, without the PDF.

> cloud content analysis is not built. Android is not covered yet.

Revert CHECK back to DCHECK in FlingCurve

In certain gesture event flows, velocity can be zero when initializing
FlingCurve, causing a CHECK macro to trigger in release builds. This
change reverts the CHECK macro back to a DCHECK until the issue is fully
investigated.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/ec09e8a888400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[Manta] Replace GetImageDescription with GetImageDescriptions

Replace the single-image Anchovy provider API with the tagged batch API
and switch `image_annotation::Annotator` to call it:

- Add `kMaxBatchSize` (`100`) in `anchovy_requests.h` and
  `AnchovyProvider::GetImageDescriptions`, `CHECK`-enforcing non-empty
  `request.images` (matching `AnchovyProtoHelper::ComposeRequest`) and
  failing fast with `kInvalidInput` without serializing image bytes or
  issuing an HTTP request when `request.images.size() > kMaxBatchSize`.
- Enforce `CHECK_GE(batch_size_, 1)` in `Annotator` and switch
  `Annotator` and `FakeAnchovyProvider` to call `GetImageDescriptions`
  with 1-image `ImageDescriptionBatchRequest`s tagged `"0"` and unpack
  `results_by_tag["0"]`.
- Remove the superseded single-image `ImageDescriptionRequest`,
  `AnchovyProtoHelper` overload/handler, and
  `AnchovyProvider::GetImageDescription` API along with superseded
  single-image unit tests, and add batched `AnchovyProviderTest` tests.

media/gpu: D3D12 video persistently map upload buffers on Intel

Work around an Intel D3D12 driver issue on buffer unmap by keeping the
video encoder's input upload buffer and the video decoder's compressed
bitstream buffer mapped for their lifetimes and clearing their first
bytes before unmapping. Gated behind the d3d12_persistent_upload_buffer_map
workaround.

tree
02238aee54dd457a54e601cb81ab76fc9a66120d
parent
5fb429894b818dfd70ed79e61e570a0a09b79c00 [ diff ] 
[Referrer-Policy] Only strip HTTP tab or space from header values

Tokens in a `Referrer-Policy` response header are separated by commas
and HTTP tab or space (0x09 HTAB and 0x20 SP) characters. Currently,
both `net::ReferrerPolicyFromHeader` and
`blink::SecurityPolicy::ReferrerPolicyFromHeaderValue` stripped a
broader set of whitespace characters, including vertical tab
(0x0B VT) and form feed (0x0C FF). This CL aligns both with the spec.

parent
9b31530b69dbf09f682b11d0186796026c32bd57 [ diff ] 
Handle ORB kSniffMore decision in WebBundleURLLoaderFactory

In WebBundleURLLoaderFactory::SendResponseToLoader, when
orb::ResponseAnalyzer::Init returned Decision::kSniffMore, the switch
statement previously fell through to Decision::kAllow and delivered the
inner response without inspecting the payload.

Update SendResponseToLoader to handle Decision::kSniffMore by reading up
to net::kMaxBytesToSniff bytes from the BundleDataSource before starting
response delivery, and evaluating the buffered bytes via
orb::ResponseAnalyzer::Sniff and HandleEndOfSniffableResponseBody.

Also abort pending BundleDataSource reads and dropped ParseResponse
callbacks on bundle error or factory destruction, and ensure orb_state_
outlives source_ so pending read callbacks owning an
orb::ResponseAnalyzer do not outlive PerFactoryState.

c289e0e63768060a855ff9ec7ecfce0e02fd7e05
parent
975e34ac3c65061dab6e812b2038b13eb4998124 [ diff ] 
[ink-overflow] Invalidate `::first-line` overflow on style changes

`LayoutBlockFlow::SetShouldDoFullPaintInvalidationForFirstLine()`
previously invalidated ink overflow only on first-line descendant items,
leaving `kLine` self ink overflow and container visual overflow stale on
paint-only `::first-line` style updates.

Invalidate `kLine` ink overflow and container visual overflow across
fragments using `::first-line style`, align
`LayoutBlock::NearestInnerBlockWithFirstLine()` with
`FirstLineStyleParentBlock()`, and trigger first-line invalidation on
inherited `::first-line` background mutations in
`LayoutObject::ApplyFirstLineChanges()`.

19855377ccbae0968da77f7eb764396d3e0b92b0 [ diff ] 
[mp-prefs] Add empty ProfilePreferenceKeys registry

Per-profile SharedPreferences keys need their own PreferenceKeyRegistry
so that StrictPreferenceKeyChecker can verify in debug builds and tests
that per-profile keys are accessed through ProfileSharedPreferences and
global keys through ChromeSharedPreferences.

This CL:
- Introduces ProfilePreferenceKeys with empty key/prefix lists and adds
  ProfilePreferenceKeys.REGISTRY to
  AllPreferenceKeyRegistries.KNOWN_REGISTRIES.
- Moves ChromeSharedPreferences.REGISTRY's active legacy-format keys and
  prefixes to ChromePreferenceKeys.getLegacyKeysInUse() and
  getLegacyPrefixesInUse(), matching ProfilePreferenceKeys.
- Turns LegacyChromePreferenceKeys into a test-only, OWNERS-gated
  allowlist of grandfathered key strings and prefix patterns (string
  literals, like DeprecatedChromePreferenceKeys). ChromePreferenceKeysTest
  now asserts that every registry's legacy keys and prefixes are in the
  allowlist, and that every allowlist entry is in use or deprecated.
- Adds per-file ProfilePreferenceKeys.java=* to OWNERS so feature owners
  can move existing keys (including legacy ones) to ProfilePreferenceKeys
  alongside their component migrations, while adding a brand-new
  unformatted key or prefix still requires preferences/OWNERS approval.
- Extends CheckNoNewSharedPreferencesCounters in PRESUBMIT.py to also
  cover ProfilePreferenceKeys.java, diffing the union of constants across
  both files so moving existing keys does not warn.

Design: go/clank-mp-sharedpreferences §5.2
Behaviour change for existing callers: none. The registry is empty and
the Chrome registry contents are unchanged.

       chrome/browser/preferences/android/PRESUBMIT_test.py

Tests: preferences_junit_tests (ChromePreferenceKeysTest),
Bug: 565218038

[Masonry] Prepare grid-lanes subgrid item groups for regular grid sizing

Prepare grouped virtual items for children of grid-lanes subgrids
contributing to regular grid track sizing.

Collect groups per axis when appending subgridded children, using the
parent's resolved placement. Group by span, baseline group and candidate
bounds—the inherited parent-track range in which automatic children can
be placed.

Prepare real-item geometry after grouping, then create virtual
candidates sharing each group's contribution sizes. Recognition remains
disabled; measurement and sizing integration will be added in a
follow-up CL.

f5eb187c3478d6eaf7bb6bdaa25f11345f625c67

Tue Nov 17 06:26:44 2026
tree
aabb8e31d6b996c3813077c4ea19acb95fb697a6
parent
f13b600cc219f575c0fec1516f381a0e2d1d379f [ diff ] 
Linux: Keep "MimeType=" out of other .desktop file values

xdg-desktop-menu, which installs web app and URL shortcut desktop
files, collects the MIME types to register as defaults by searching
every line of the file for "MimeType=", not only the MimeType key.
Names and URLs written to other keys can come from web content.

Break up "MimeType=" wherever it appears outside the MimeType key:
percent-encode it in Exec arguments (which keeps URLs equivalent) and
insert a zero width space elsewhere.

xdg-mime's fallback lookup, used only for types with no registered
handler, also matches MIME types outside the MimeType key. That is an
xdg-utils issue and is not addressed here.

f13b600cc219f575c0fec1516f381a0e2d1d379f

Helmut Januschka <helmut@januschka.com>

Tue Nov 17 06:24:58 2026
tree
bbd3476192467d4fb5600017fd6ff872e54a04df
parent
648565bb15bc7de7ed4a487e129afb57cac89dce [ diff ] 
Paint border-shape outlines along the shape for all outline styles

BorderShapePainter::PaintOutline only handled solid and double outline
styles; every other style returned false and fell through to the
rectangular outline painter, so a border-shape element with a dotted,
dashed, inset, outset, groove or ridge outline drew a box-shaped
outline instead of one that followed the shape.

Extend PaintOutline to cover the remaining styles. Dotted and dashed
outlines stroke the center of the outline band with StyledStrokeData,
clipped to the band so an oversized stroke stays inside it. Inset,
outset, groove and ridge fill the band (or its outer and inner halves)
with the outline color and its darkened variant, which is the only
well-defined split for an arbitrary border-shape.

parent
dceee087beda430b5ddf7ca7525870a6b33bf614 [ diff ] 
[Transit] Split Public Transit targets out of base/test/BUILD.gn

gn analyze tracks .gn files at whole-file granularity rather than per if
(is_android) block. Because //base/test/BUILD.gn defines
//base/test:test_support, adding or removing a Public Transit Java file
dirties test_support across every platform and triggers ~100
desktop/mobile test suites (such as win-rel and mac-rel) as well as
unrelated C++ gtests on Android.

Move public_transit_java and public_transit_robolectric_tests_java into
a dedicated BUILD.gn in the transit directory, leaving java_group()
forwarding aliases in //base/test/BUILD.gn so existing callers remain
unchanged while future file list edits stay isolated from
//base/test/BUILD.gn.

Reland "Update last committed service worker client on navigation commit"

This is a reland of commit a6cafef0c22248a7f359b9941f52a44d98a43442.

Difference from original CL:
Disable `features::kOriginKeyedProcessesByDefault` in the test fixture
(`ServiceWorkerNoOriginKeyedProcessesByDefaultBrowserTest`) for
`BlobWorkerCreatedDuringUncommittedNavigationDoesNotInheritController`
and `LastCommittedServiceWorkerClientUpdatedOnlyAfterCommit`, since both
tests specifically test reusing an initial empty iframe RenderFrameHost
across a same-site, cross-origin navigation (which otherwise swaps to a
speculative RenderFrameHost on `linux-oi-rel`).

Original change's description:
> Update last committed service worker client on navigation commit
>
> Previously, RenderFrameHostImpl::AddServiceWorkerClient() updated
> last_committed_service_worker_client_ when preparing to commit the
> navigation response. When a RenderFrameHost was reused across
> cross-document navigations, this allowed GetLastCommittedServiceWorkerClient()
> to return the pending navigation's client while the previous document
> remained active.
>
> Defer updating last_committed_service_worker_client_ until the document
> commit completes and new document properties are applied, while checking
> the committing NavigationRequest's GetCommittedServiceWorkerClient()
> when computing ResourceBroker routing decisions during CommitNavigation.
> In addition, verify that the parent/creator origin matches the client's
> storage key origin before inheriting the service worker controller.
>
> Bug: 581369093

317cd051fbec53d36cdcf5fd5292e08f82bf95b6
parent
53cd8f41b18b51a759a47fb28c75e95a5cdd2708 [ diff ] 
[WebNN] Broadcast matmul inputs on GPU to match output batch dimensions

The LiteRT ML Drift GPU delegate's BATCH_MATMUL operation does not
broadcast batch dimensions and maps lower-rank tensors left-aligned into
BHWC, which can cause out-of-bounds GPU shader reads when inputs have
mismatched ranks or broadcastable batch dimensions of extent 1.

When targeting the GPU device and the output rank fits within BHWC
(<= 4D), explicitly broadcast `a` and `b` to the output batch dimensions
using `SerializeBroadcastToAsReshapeAndTile` before emitting
`BATCH_MATMUL`, while preserving the 2D constant RHS fast path when `a`
is a runtime tensor.

591138f670893580554e5be90babc68c7c8ce2a8 [ diff ] 
[On-Device Model] Surface context window exceeded errors from Generate()

When a session's context window (KV cache) is full, the ChromeML
library has no way to report it today: it either crashes the on-device
model service or reports a successful, empty response.

Add ChromeMLGenerateStatus::kContextWindowExceeded and a matching
mojom::GenerateError::kContextWindowExceeded. The service reports it the
same way as kInvalidConstraint, by closing the responder pipe with the
error as the reason.

Callers map the new error as follows:
- AILanguageModel:
  ModelStreamingResponseStatus::kErrorResponseExceedsRemainingContext.
- OnDeviceExecution: Result::kFailedConstructingMessage with
  OnDeviceError::kInvalidRequest, like kInvalidConstraint.

A follow-up change in the ChromeML library ends requests with the new
status instead of crashing.

[Chrome CodeHealth] Remove obsolete Config::use_host_for_visit_deduping

Following the removal of `kHistoryClustersVisitDeduping`[1],
`Config::use_host_for_visit_deduping` is permanently `true` in

clear the URL path in `ComputeURLForDeduping()`, and update the affected
unit tests to use host-based deduping.

No behavior change.

Reland "WebNN: Copy compiled CoreML model directly without base::Move"

This is a reland of commit 246a3dd98708a33199784720a97066eb736bd2a3

Why it was reverted:
On `mac14-arm64-rel-tests`, Swarming sets `MAC_CHROMIUM_TMPDIR` on a
separate volume (`/Volumes/Work/...`) from CoreML's `compileModelAtURL:`
output directory (`/var/folders/...`). In the original CL,
`CompilerContextImplCoreml` pre-created `<scoped_dir_A>` via
`CreateUniqueTempDirUnderPath` before calling
`base::Move(raw_compiled_model_path, compiled_model_dir.GetPath())`.
When `rename()` succeeded on the same volume, it replaced the empty
`<scoped_dir_A>` directory (`<scoped_dir_A>/coremldata.bin`); when
`rename()` failed with `EXDEV` across volumes, `base::Move` fell back to
`base::CopyDirectory`, which saw that `<scoped_dir_A>` already existed
and copied `model.mlmodelc` as a subdirectory inside it
(`<scoped_dir_A>/model.mlmodelc/coremldata.bin`), resulting in a double
`model.mlmodelc/model.mlmodelc` after `CopyCompiledModel`.

What changed in reland:
Move `raw_compiled_model_path` to a non-existent
`webnn_compiler_protected/<token>` path and adopt it into
`ScopedTempDir` via `Set()`, so both same-volume `rename()` and
cross-volume `CopyDirectory()` place the compiled model contents
directly at `webnn_compiler_protected/<token>`.

Original change's description:
> WebNN: Copy compiled CoreML model directly without base::Move
>
> Have the compiler process move CoreML's compiled output directly to
> `$TMPDIR/webnn_compiler_protected/<scoped_dir_A>` without an extra
> `model.mlmodelc` subdirectory. The browser process can then copy
> `<scoped_dir_A>` directly to
> `$TMPDIR/webnn_gpu_protected/<scoped_dir_B>/model.mlmodelc` using
> `base::CopyDirectoryNoFollow`, avoiding `base::Move` (which falls back
> to symlink-following `base::CopyDirectory` on failure) and
> `base::DeletePathRecursively`.
>
> Bug: 581913018

cb7669fbca6d3b1d70d5d436cb2d79700b94775b [ diff ] 
[TopChrome] Move WebUI process reuse to //content and remove hook

After exposing `WebUIConfigMap::GetProcessLockHost()`
(https://crrev.com/c/8752739),
`ChromeContentBrowserClient::ShouldTryToUseExistingProcessHost()` is the
only production override of
`ContentBrowserClient::ShouldTryToUseExistingProcessHost()` and only
queries `WebUIConfigMap::GetProcessLockHost()`. The hook was originally
added for extensions, but that use was removed in
https://crrev.com/c/3169415, leaving Top Chrome WebUIs as its only user.

This CL moves that check directly into
`RenderProcessHostImpl::GetProcessHostForSiteInstance()` in `//content/`
and removes the
`ContentBrowserClient::ShouldTryToUseExistingProcessHost()` embedder
hook. Because shared-lock WebUIs now reuse an existing renderer process
directly in `//content/`:
- `SharedProcessLockDifferentSiteInstance*` in
  `web_ui_navigation_browsertest.cc` now expect process reuse, while
  `NoProcessLockHostDoesNotShareProcess` keeps covering WebUIs without a
  process lock host.
- The new `DifferentProcessLockHostsDoNotShareProcess` verifies that
  WebUIs with different process lock hosts do not share a process.
- `ShouldReuseRendererWhenTopChromePagesPresent` moves from
  `chrome_content_browser_client_browsertest.cc` to
  `chrome_webui_navigation_browsertest.cc` and also verifies that
  non-Top Chrome WebUIs do not share a process.
- The mock client in `spare_render_process_host_manager_browsertest.cc`
  uses `ShouldUseProcessPerSite()` instead of the removed hook.

Use current_frame_host() origin when sanitizing error redirects
📋 Metadata
commit
cb7669fbca6d3b1d70d5d436cb2d79700b94775b

Rakina Zata Amni <rakina@chromium.org>

Tue Nov 17 05:30:30 2026
tree
4a576453bad6b335cd601e8cb6bd046e4c212e94
parent
8b5d40b10da677932b491dd968a7b67faba2f168 [ diff ] 
Use current_frame_host() origin when sanitizing error redirects

When an error page commits in ErrorPageProcess::kCurrentProcess,
NavigationRequest::SanitizeRedirectsForCommit() preserves redirects
that are same-origin with the current document in that process.
Previously it checked GetRenderFrameHost()->GetLastCommittedOrigin(),
which returns an uncommitted opaque origin when GetRenderFrameHost() is
a newly created speculative RenderFrameHost in the same process,
causing same-origin redirects to be sanitized out.

Relation to RenderDocument: Without RenderDocument, failed navigations
committing an error page in ErrorPageProcess::kCurrentProcess reuse
current_frame_host(), which holds the current document's committed
origin. With RenderDocument, same-process cross-document navigations
target a fresh speculative RenderFrameHost whose
GetLastCommittedOrigin() is still an uncommitted opaque origin. This
affects 2nd+ subframe navigations on trunk with RenderDocument
(all-frames), and also affects 1st subframe navigations once
RenderDocument is enabled for initial empty documents.

Check frame_tree_node_->current_frame_host()->GetLastCommittedOrigin()
(asserting that GetRenderFrameHost() shares current_frame_host()'s
process) so same-origin redirects are preserved across same-process
RenderFrameHost swaps.

TAG=agy
CONV=d8ac1d3b-b6ed-444b-8f2e-27b4a4ae916e

tree
3cfc5600bcf2ae747950becbc866686bbe7cc7ee
parent
8dc9c6c900cf915950625a7963db2ce2f81d753f [ diff ] 
variations: Don't write .variations-list.txt in CrOS child processes

Android and ChromeOS are the only platforms where VariationsCrashKeys
writes active variations to disk. Unlike Android, ChromeOS writes from
all processes without canceling superseded tasks, scheduling 176 tasks
(~958 ms of I/O) during login, including 38 writes from unsandboxed
utility processes.

Pass ProcessType to InitCrashKeys() so only the browser process writes
to disk on both platforms (replacing Android's "type" switch check),
and use CancelableTaskTracker on ChromeOS to cancel superseded writes.

[Chrome CodeHealth] Remove stale HistoryClustersVisitDeduping feature

The feature was disabled by default, so its only param lookup
(`use_host_for_visit_deduping`) always fell back to the Config default
of `true`, which has been the default behavior since M111
(crrev.com/c/4177842). Remove the feature and the field trial param
lookup. The `use_host_for_visit_deduping` Config field is kept since
unit tests still use it to exercise path-preserving deduping. It can be
removed in a followup CL.

No behavior change.

Use WeakPtr in MirroringActivity session startup

The asynchronous ShouldFetchMirroringStats response can arrive after
MirroringActivity has been destroyed. Bind the StartSession callback
using the existing WeakPtrFactory instead of base::Unretained(this) to
prevent a use-after-free when a response arrives after teardown.

Mojo delivers the response on the owning sequence, so weak-pointer
binding is safe. Remove the obsolete comment about cross-sequence
dereferencing.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in AXNode::GetParentCrossingTreeBoundary

AXNode::GetParentCrossingTreeBoundary can be called during tree
unserialization while a tree update is in progress. A CHECK asserting
that a tree update is not in progress causes crashes during tree
unserialization.

Reverting this assertion back to DCHECK resolves the crash while tree
updates are in progress.

e86e78266017a3f60dff54ec56f5cd11f601ae73
parent
84caee1fb683ff9f81523e3e1296c022494a391e [ diff ] 
[Reading mode PDFs] Map image AXNodeIDs to PDF page object indices

When PdfAccessibilityTreeBuilder creates an image accessibility node
(ui::AXNodeData), it assigns a synthetic renderer-generated
ui::AXNodeID. When Reading Mode later requests the bitmap for a specific
image AXNodeID, the PDF subsystem has no way to resolve that node ID
back to the underlying PDF page and PDFium page object index, both of
which PDFium requires (via FPDFPage_GetObject()) to locate and extract
the image bitmap.

Record a reverse mapping from image AXNodeID to {page_index,
page_object_index} in PdfAccessibilityTree when building image nodes in
PdfAccessibilityTreeBuilder::CreateImageNode(), and expose it via
PdfAccessibilityTree::GetImageInfo() for use in a follow-up CL. Clear
the mapping in ClearAccessibilityNodes() on document reloads.

This change is part of the broader effort to support rendering PDF
raster images in Reading Mode (see go/rm-pdf-raster-images for context).

Tue Nov 17 05:02:13 2026
tree
75bfc222dbbd2a7e55687d0dd41fba85ada1734b
parent
f7a472b48d935ba5e892fde869854a4bb49b51cc [ diff ] 
[FSA] Treat user-sourced ALLOW on File System Access guards as ASK

This CL makes `GetReadGuardContentSetting()` and
`GetWriteGuardContentSetting()` in
`ChromeFileSystemAccessPermissionContext` return `CONTENT_SETTING_ASK`
when `FILE_SYSTEM_READ_GUARD` / `FILE_SYSTEM_WRITE_GUARD` resolve to
`CONTENT_SETTING_ALLOW` from `SettingSource::kUser`, and overrides
`CanRequestObjectPermission()` so persisted grants stay visible for
such origins.

Previously, a user-sourced `ALLOW` was honored and every handle for the
origin became `GRANTED` without a prompt, even though Chrome UI and
enterprise policy only ever set `ASK` or `BLOCK` for these guards
(`ALLOW` is produced only by allowlist providers such as WebUI and
DevTools). A user-sourced `ALLOW` can therefore only come from an
unvalidated `Preferences` file written outside Chrome. Checking the
sensitive-entry blocklist on deserialized handles is handled in a
separate CL.

[Webium] Don't bind Live Caption interfaces in TopChromeWebUIProfile
📋 Metadata
commit
7455227969dc07121a1688bb3c1be8e2dd59813b
[ log ]
author
Anna Sato <annasato@chromium.org>
Tue Nov 17 04:50:16 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 17 04:50:16 2026
tree
7d671083da445fcf73e170c8e94214bcfb065b71
parent
6f03a16663ed63e64e6428807d04797f3f1ba7de [ diff ] 
[Webium] Don't bind Live Caption interfaces in TopChromeWebUIProfile

With kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer, the AI Mode omnibox popup is
created in TopChromeWebUIProfile, which is shared by the windows of all
profiles and has no keyed services or Live Caption prefs. When voice
search starts listening, the recording wave animation creates an
AudioContext to read the microphone levels, which creates a Live Caption
speech recognition client in the renderer. The browser then binds
SpeechRecognitionClientBrowserInterface to the service of the profile of
the frame, which is null, and crashes.

The popup plays no audio, so it has nothing to caption. Don't bind
SpeechRecognitionClientBrowserInterface for a frame in a profile with
keyed services disabled by default, like TopChromeWebUIProfile. Without
it, the renderer never learns that Live Caption is available, so it
doesn't request the other Live Caption interfaces. Speech recognition
for voice search itself uses other interfaces.

Other binders in PopulateChromeFrameBinders() can also hit null keyed
services or unregistered prefs in TopChromeWebUIProfile, but only when a
WebUI uses their APIs. Ideally TopChromeWebUIProfile should only expose
the binders that Top Chrome WebUIs need, which will be done separately.
This CL only fixes the crash that voice search hits today.

040f2dce03f4abccf6bcb1a5ff75583462095ed9
parent
12295f9f91be2e71e5a6d0e31f5ba3004087a152 [ diff ] 
Ash: Move lock screen reauth media access to MediaCaptureDispatcher

This CL is part of an ongoing series to remove Ash code's dependency
on MediaCaptureDevicesDispatcher, a //chrome singleton, by moving Ash
callers to an interface in components/webrtc that the dispatcher
implements.

When a SAML identity provider's page asks to use the camera while the
user re-authenticates on the lock screen, the reauth dialog passes the
request to the dispatcher, which allows it only for sites the device
policy lists. This CL makes LockScreenStartReauthDialog use
MediaCaptureDispatcher, so the reauth dialog no longer depends on
//chrome/browser/media/webrtc.

[emoji] Generate emoji data in Python using 8-bit `UCPTrie`

This patch:
1. Replaces the inline Emacs Lisp in `character_emoji.cc` with
  `generate_character_emoji_data.py`, which fetches
   `emoji-data.txt` and emits `character_emoji_data.h`.
   The generator accepts an optional Unicode version.
2. Uses a `UCPTrie` generated at the build time, instead of
   multiple `UnicodeSet`s at the runtime, when using Blink's
   Emoji data.

There should be no behavior changes. The data isn't updated in
this patch (currently 17.0.0).

ba0ad97d219a17015217e2c6b22017d1d16afebe [ diff ] 
[remoting host] Add display observers and snap-back to PrivacyOverlayMac

Observes macOS display, window movement, and accessibility contrast
notifications in `PrivacyOverlayMac`:

- Adds `PrivacyOverlayNotificationObserver` to listen for
  `NSApplicationDidChangeScreenParametersNotification`,
  `NSWindowDidMoveNotification`, and
  `NSWorkspaceAccessibilityDisplayOptionsDidChangeNotification`.
- Rebuilds/resizes per-display backdrop windows, repositions
  `button_window_`, and resets reposition attempts on display changes.
- Treats CGS capture exclusion on `button_window_` as best-effort
  (`LOG(WARNING)`) while keeping `backdrop_windows_` fail-closed.
- Snaps `button_window_` back to its expected coordinates when displaced
  (bounded by `DisconnectWindowBase::kMaxRepositionAttempts`).
- Marks backdrop and button views `needsDisplay` when macOS Increase
  Contrast accessibility settings change.
- Adds unit tests for displacement snap-back with loop guard, screen
  parameter updates (including re-entrant observer teardown when
  backdrop updates fail and best-effort button capture exclusion), and
  accessibility contrast redraws.

Revert CHECK back to DCHECK in AXPosition::AsLeafTextPosition

Under certain conditions, AsLeafTextPosition() can produce a null text
position. Reverting the CHECK macro back to a DCHECK prevents fatal
crashes in release builds while allowing further investigation into when
null text positions occur.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/ca27a2b8af800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

tree
5bf94af554d32ecf60cafc84b8c83eb046407b3b
parent
41d1fad09fc3b535112b24875d246ef2945aa9bd [ diff ] 
[libzip] Use _snprintf_s on Windows

config.h defined HAVE_SNPRINTF_S on Windows, but the Windows CRT only
provides _snprintf_s, not the C11 Annex K snprintf_s. The calls in
zip_error_strerror.c and zip_source_file_stdio_named.c only compiled
because BUILD.gn passes -Wno-implicit-function-declaration. C23 removed
implicit function declarations, so they now fail with "use of
undeclared identifier 'snprintf_s'".

Define HAVE__SNPRINTF_S instead, so that compat.h maps snprintf_s to
_snprintf_s.

This prepares for building C as C23.

0064a78cf1d8ee9a1c246bcb808f0ce714ef3b7c
parent
d1e8132426c903490799cf8bb55d267cfea9340b [ diff ] 
base: Add value-returning Base64UrlEncode() overloads

base/base64.h provides std::string-returning Base64Encode() overloads
for span<const uint8_t> and std::string_view, and base/base64url.h
provides a value-returning Base64UrlDecode() overload, but
Base64UrlEncode() previously only had out-parameter overloads. Most
callers ended up declaring a single-use temporary std::string just to
pass &output.

Add value-returning Base64UrlEncode() overloads for span<const uint8_t>
and std::string_view, keeping the existing out-parameter overloads as
thin wrappers. Factor the '+/' -> '-_' substitution and padding
stripping, previously duplicated in Base64UrlEncodeEarlyStartup(), into
a shared Base64ToBase64Url() helper.

Also migrate callers in chrome/browser/ash and chromeos/ash (dropping
intermediate copies or reinterpret_casts where callers already hold
byte spans). No behavior change is intended.

[🌷] Deflake TabbedModeTabModelStoreTest activity recreation tests

Wait for the previous ChromeTabbedActivity to reach Stage.DESTROYED and
for the newly recreated activity to finish native and compositor
initialization in recreateActivityAndWait() so posted UI startup tasks
(such as TopToolbarOverlayMediator's ObservationScope) do not outlive
test teardown. Also verify asynchronous TabStateStorageService and
ActiveTabCache writes have completed before recreating the activity.

Additionally, remove temporary RESTRICTION_TYPE_NON_AUTO restrictions
added during gardener triage (crbug.com/567031910, crbug.com/565238230)
now that the underlying activity recreation flake is resolved.

Revert CHECK_EQ to DCHECK_EQ for custom view position in StyledLabel

When measuring or laying out a StyledLabel containing a custom view
across line boundaries or during size calculation, position can be
greater than range.start(), triggering a fatal CHECK_EQ.

This change reverts the CHECK_EQ back to DCHECK_EQ with a TODO comment
to convert back once confident it will not be triggered.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/47d0f0e4af800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[ash] Accessibility: Track the AccountId together with the profile

AccessibilityManager used multi_user_util::GetAccountIdFromProfile()
to map its current Profile back to the owning user's AccountId when
writing KnownUser prefs (spoken feedback, startup sound, and bluetooth
braille display address).

The profile is set either from a User (SetProfileByUser(), on active
user change once the user's profile is created), to the signin profile
when there is no active session, or to null on profile destruction.
Keep the AccountId along with the profile: take it from the User in
SetProfileByUser(), and leave it empty for the signin profile and for
null. Use it for the KnownUser writes instead of the Profile to User
lookup.

Note that UserManager::GetActiveUser() cannot be used instead, because
the active user is switched before the new user's profile is set here,
so it could write to another user's KnownUser entry.

The IsUserBrowserContext(profile_) check in OnSpokenFeedbackChanged()
is replaced with checking whether the AccountId is valid, which is
equivalent because only user profiles come with an AccountId. For the
signin profile, the previous code resolved an empty AccountId, which
made the KnownUser writes no-ops, so the behavior is unchanged.

SetProfileForTest() takes an optional AccountId so that a test passing
a user profile can provide the owning user.

With this change, //chrome/browser/ash/accessibility no longer
includes multi_user_util.h, so //chrome/browser/ui/ash/multi_user no
longer needs to depend on it nor allow circular includes from it.

AX-Relnotes: n/a.

Revert CHECK_GE back to DCHECK_GE in GetMainAndroidPackFd

When GetMainAndroidPackFd is called before or without the main android
resource pack file descriptor being initialized, g_resources_pack_fd is
less than 0, causing the CHECK_GE macro to fail and trigger a crash.

This converts CHECK_GE back to DCHECK_GE with a TODO comment to avoid
crashing when g_resources_pack_fd is uninitialized.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/e178156888400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Mark Pickle::header_ UnprotectedInReleaseForPerformance

remove_unprotected_in_release_trait gives raw_ptr fields marked
UnprotectedInRelease BackupRefPtr protection in release builds. Fields
marked UnprotectedInReleaseForPerformance stay unprotected.

Pickle::Resize() reassigns header_ on every reallocation, which
releases and acquires a BackupRefPtr reference each time. This shows up
as about 9% of samples in tracing_perftests
TraceEventPerfTest.Create_10000_TracedValue, which regressed by 25% on
linux-r350-perf.

Mark header_ UnprotectedInReleaseForPerformance.

9382e67982fe41307d4fac880987fa7b13e5942f [ diff ] 
[grpc] Fix upb compile errors under C23

- message/copy.c, text/internal/encode.c: functions returning a
  pointer did `return false;`. In C23 `false` is a bool constant, which
  trips -Wbool-conversion. Return NULL instead, as upstream protobuf
  does.
- message/internal/message.c: clang can't fold `1.0 / 0.0` and
  `0.0 / 0.0` into static initializers in C23 mode ("cannot compile
  this static initializer yet"). Use upstream's UPB_NAN/UPB_INFINITY
  (NAN/INFINITY, or __builtin_nan/__builtin_inf on Windows).

These match upstream grpc 5a18d66e23bf ("[Deps] Updated Protobuf to
v30"); the patch file notes it can be dropped once a roll includes it.

The changes are also recorded in
patches/0005-upb-c23-fixes.patch so update.sh reapplies them on the
next roll.

This prepares for building C as C23.

Skip dangling pointer detection during Oilpan GC finalization
📋 Metadata
commit
9382e67982fe41307d4fac880987fa7b13e5942f
[ log ]
author
Keishi Hattori <keishi@google.com>
Tue Nov 17 04:06:33 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 17 04:06:33 2026
tree
073db333982dfdb9c8c2c15f5db21f3b2cfe0654
parent
07dcaac6d0a7020e0154d1e9b93ecb023cf6838e [ diff ] 
Skip dangling pointer detection during Oilpan GC finalization

The dangling pointer detector (DPD) remembers when memory is freed
while a raw_ptr still points to it, and reports when that raw_ptr is
later cleared or destroyed. It runs on bots with
enable_dangling_raw_ptr_checks.

When Oilpan object A owns a native allocation B, Oilpan object C has
a raw_ptr to B, and a GC finds both unreachable:
1. A's finalizer frees B.
2. C's finalizer destroys the raw_ptr. DPD reports.
The order of 1 and 2 depends on pre-finalizer registration order and
sweeping page order, so the report is flaky. It is also harmless: C
is unreachable, so nothing can dereference the raw_ptr in between.
Today such fields are marked DanglingUntriaged, which also disables
detection in mutator code.

Add ThreadState::IsInGcFinalization(), which is true during
sweeping, weak processing, pre-finalizers, heap termination, and
isolate disposal, and register it with DPD. DPD calls it only when
it is about to report.

TAG=agy
CONV=4804c1d9-08b3-4292-9f5b-6a213106eb4c

8e9d6d46f7963403470e52c6bbfecc27217e05d7
[ log ]
author
Kent Tamura <tkent@chromium.org>

committer

Tue Nov 17 04:01:54 2026
tree
3cb3083ffdf59229ee483bcbe971b868bb8ac316
parent
5aae16906d763d41b07e8feada001125ffa98e4a [ diff ] 
Use StringImpl::g_global_empty* directly in blink::StringView

StringImpl::empty_ and StringImpl::empty16_bit_ are out-of-line
constinit pointer variables defined in string_impl.cc, so inlined
StringView constructors and StringView::Clear() had to load the pointer
value from memory at each call site.

Using &StringImpl::g_global_empty and &StringImpl::g_global_empty16_bit
directly lets the compiler reference the static objects' addresses
without an extra pointer load, reducing code size at inlined call sites.
This reduces the browser binary size by 24 KiB on desktop platforms.

No behavior changes.

[Graphite] Skip crash dumps for Dawn OOM errors

Dawn allocation failures can reflect external, OS resource pressure
rather than a Chromium programming error. Avoid generating nonfatal
crash dumps for OOM errors while preserving logging, crash keys, UMA,
and context-loss handling.

Validation and other unexpected errors retain their existing dump
reporting behavior.

Revert "Fix lifecycle and disconnection crashes during WebPagePopup initialization"

This reverts commit 344a27419e07208818730ebd258f7aa03df4d5e7.

Reason for revert:
Causes failure of `InternalPopupMenuTest.DisconnectDuringPopupConstruction` in `blink_unittests` on `Linux Tests` bot.

Failure is:
```
internal_popup_menu_test.cc(1224): Expected equality of these values:
  1
  blur_listener->BlurCount()
    Which is: 0
```

First failing bot run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Linux%20Tests/190671/test-results

Original change's description:
> Fix lifecycle and disconnection crashes during WebPagePopup initialization
>
> During WebPagePopup initialization and forced layout updates,
> synchronous author script or nested run loops can trigger Mojo
> disconnection or close the popup re-entrantly. If the owner element is
> modified or disconnected mid- creation, accessing OwnerElement() could
> crash or leave dangling references.
>
> This CL handles these edge cases by:
> - Adding HasOwnerElement() to PagePopupClient and its implementations to
>   safely check element availability before dereferencing OwnerElement().
> - Splitting WebPagePopupImpl construction and initialization, and
>   tracking an explicit State (kOpen, kClosing, kClosed) rather than a
>   simple boolean.
> - Pre-computing the owner bounding rect and properly handling
>   disconnections or re-entrant popup openings during
>   WebPagePopupImpl::Initialize().
> - Ensuring WebViewImpl verifies opener frame and document state after
>   style and layout updates, cleanly abandoning popup creation if
>   invalidated.
>

d213c8603474428e6239bac2ca7960eb5e21ab3c [ diff ] 
Android: Remove Monochrome-specific locale asset paths

assets/stored-locales/ was used by monochrome for strings used by
webview but not chrome, to prevent them from being moved to locale
splits.

Monochrome is gone, so this simplifies the paths to use the normal
* assets/fallback-locales/
* assets/locales#lang_<lang>/

Locale splits are avoided on webview by disabling them for the bundle
(already the case).

Exclude locale .pak subpaths when applying Chrome's custom
TrichromeLibrary asset-collision suffix (crbug.com/40064988) so Chrome's
locale .pak assets remain unsuffixed, and remove the now-unused inSplit,
stored-locales, treat_as_locale_paks, and Monochrome NonWebView locale
.pak plumbing.

Revert caps lock check in MacKeyCodeForWindowsKeyCode back to DCHECK

In MacKeyCodeForWindowsKeyCode, checking that caps lock is not set was
recently converted to a fatal CHECK. However, caps lock being set can be
triggered in release builds when caps lock is enabled, causing crashes.
Revert this check back to a DCHECK_EQ and add a CHECK-exclusion comment.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/63f7bde208400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

tree
40f6010cfbda621946929964102d70b6c49189bd
parent
1407a6c21b0bf6189f6706fc6ee341e5c1e4e78c [ diff ] 
[ink-overflow] Include `::first-line` overflow in line-box occlusion

`LineBoxFragmentPainter::PaintBackgroundBorderShadow()` paints
`::first-line` box decorations in `PaintPhase::kForeground`, and paints
them even when the containing block has `visibility: hidden` if
`::first-line` sets `visibility: visible`. Relying only on container
self-background ink overflow misses occlusion in those cases and against
same-stacking floats or in-flow blocks.

Include painted `::first-line` ink overflow (with container filters,
block-in-inline, float ordering, and anonymous block node resolution)
during `kHitTestVisualOverflow` checks in
`BoxFragmentPainter::HitTestLineBoxFragment()`, while keeping ordinary
pointer hit-testing unchanged.

tree
b3dc0596256bb2f191e5fd754345690063a1bdab
parent
ee0cf527d8e6aa48df64924bc982cf79cd575e79 [ diff ] 
[Full WebUI Omnibox] Restore omnibox focus and selection on tab drag

When `WebUIOmniboxFullPopup` is enabled, dragging a tab with a focused
omnibox out into a new window and back restored the omnibox with all
text selected instead of the user's selection, because the selection
lived only in the WebUI and was dropped whenever the WebUI hadn't been
sent the tab's state, and passive syncs fell back to select-all.

This change makes the Full WebUI omnibox mirror `OmniboxViewViews`:
`OmniboxPopupViewFullWebUI` saves the selection and unelided state in
`OnBlur()`, persists it in `OmniboxState` via `SaveStateToTab()` and
`OnTabChanged()` (holding it until a new window's WebUI is ready), and
preserves it on syncs while focused. `LocationBarView::OnFocus()`
restores it instead of selecting all, except on focus traversal. Like
`OmniboxViewViews`, a new native selection, a direct click into the
WebUI input, or a cross-document navigation drops the saved selection.

8094fdabda03ed729acab61dfc0d58bf80070cbe

df9f5be257a16e9f92e9da4a4b14ea04aefe4716 [ diff ] 
Android: Disable expectation verification on x64

No *.x64.libs_and_assets.expected files exist for chrome_public_bundle
or the system_webview bundles, so enabling the check on x64 would point
at non-existent expectation files. Manifest expectations would also not
match on x64 since android:versionCode offsets differ and OpenXR is
disabled there. This reverts the expectations.gni part of
ad07dce4c5cdd, which enabled expectation checks on x64 for the
android-desktop-x64 binary-size builder (which no longer builds
validate_expectations).

2dab670b882956eceed8ef0f3eaaf6a24335665e [ diff ] 
[iOS] Show History Sync promo after Account Switching promo

According to the PRD, a switch through the account switch promo must
behave like a switch through the account menu. That means, we need to
potentially show the history sync promo once it’s done. So we must show
the AccountSwitchPromo through the signin and history sync coordinator.
This CL add the needed plumbing to do so.

`SignInAndHistorySyncCoordinator` gets a `preselectedIdentity`, passed
to `FullscreenSigninCoordinator`. In https://crrev.com/c/8580342,
`SceneCoordinator` creates `SignInAndHistorySyncCoordinator` directly
with the target identity of the Account Switching promo. The existing
`+signinAndHistorySyncCoordinatorWithBaseViewController:...` factory
passes `nil`, as none of its callers preselects an identity.

Since the promo is shown while a primary account is signed in,
`SignInAndHistorySyncCoordinator` now completes with the error of the
last step, unless it is `SigninCoordinatorResultDisabled` (which means
that the step was skipped). Before, only
`SigninCoordinatorResultInterrupted` was propagated, so canceling the
sign-in step reported the existing primary identity as a success.

For the same reason, `FullscreenSigninCoordinator` now finishes with
`SigninCoordinatorResultCanceledByUser` when the Account Switching promo
is dismissed without switching the primary account. Before,
`-finishWithPrimaryIdentity` reported the existing primary identity as
a success, so `SignInAndHistorySyncCoordinator` would have shown the
History Sync screen for the account that was already signed in.

Test: ios_chrome_unittests --gtest_filter=FullscreenSigninCoordinator*

parent
7e5d13cae9169b7ebbb50c7577c717842c78e1da [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Lazy-load non-initially visible elements in Full WebUI popup

The Full WebUI omnibox popup initially renders only the location bar
input row. <omnibox-popup-contextual-entrypoint> (contextual entrypoint
button, current tab / Lens chips, Lens icon) lives inside
`.dropdownContainer` and is not visible until the dropdown opens, yet
its code was pulled into omnibox_popup_full.rollup.js on the initial
critical path via an eager import.

Following the New Tab Page lazy loading pattern, this CL:

- Adds lazy_load.ts and WindowProxy (`waitForLazyRender()` and
  `ensureLazyLoaded()`), and moves the
  omnibox_popup_contextual_entrypoint.js import to lazy_load.ts.
  <cr-searchbox-compose-button> stays eager since it can be visible in
  the input row.

- Gates <omnibox-popup-contextual-entrypoint> behind `lazyRender_`,
  flipped once lazy_load.js has loaded. Loading starts after
  waitForLazyRender() in firstUpdated(), or on the first `onShow` if the
  popup opens before the idle callback fires.

- Forwards eligibility state received over Mojo before the lazy element
  connects (isAimPopupEligible, isContentSharingEnabled,
  isLensSearchEligible, isLensIconEligible) via property bindings, and
  has the contextual entrypoint fetch the current tab on connect when
  the page is visible, in case it missed the `onShow` push.

- Removes unused side-effect imports in contextual_entrypoint_button.ts
  and composebox_lens_search.ts, and makes composebox_mixin.ts use a
  type-only import of ContextualEntrypointAndMenuElement.

  lazy_load.js (8 lazy), plus unit tests for the deferred rendering and
  early Mojo state.

tree
383ff349bd8eb0ba3e7d1bf94d8d86a93e6f6a2f
parent
e04144d47cff0bda3f9ced963255061fc2f13fef [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Drag URL from and handle drops on full webui omnibox input

Before this CL, dragging the whole URL out of the full webui omnibox
input dragged only the displayed text, which may be elided, and a drop
from elsewhere was left to Blink, which inserted the raw text at the
drop point, "javascript:" scheme included.

With this change, like the Views omnibox:
- Dragging all of the unedited URL drags the page's URL, as a URL and
  as text, and allows linking. The browser sends it as the new
  OmniboxInputState.drag_url, since the displayed text can lack the
  scheme. It's left out if the popup may not drag it, e.g. for a
  view-source: URL, which is dragged as text.
- A URL or text dropped on the input replaces all of its text, with
  "javascript:" schemes stripped, is selected, and focuses the
  omnibox.

GetUrlForCopyOfUnmodifiedText() is split out of
omnibox::AdjustTextForCopy(), with no behavior change, so that the
URL is available without creating the AutocompleteClassifier.

f5fac35fbc9e28156b04c8b7d1149e82ddb96b32 [ diff ] 
Revert "Scope SharedWorkerConnectorImpl lifetime to DocumentService"

This reverts commit 7c0bfdd41b8e3570cfce347b1a258460a7ce4701.

Reason for revert: WorkerTest.SharedWorkerInPagehideDuringCrossOriginCommit is failing.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/linux-oi-rel/37366/overview

Original change's description:
> Scope SharedWorkerConnectorImpl lifetime to DocumentService
>
> SharedWorkerConnectorImpl was previously owned via
> mojo::MakeSelfOwnedReceiver and stored a GlobalRenderFrameHostId.
> Because its lifetime was tied to the Mojo pipe rather than the
> document lifecycle, Connect() requests sent during document teardown
> could be processed after a cross-document navigation committed in the
> same RenderFrameHost, resolving state against the newly committed
> document.
>
> Convert SharedWorkerConnectorImpl to inherit from
> content::DocumentService so the receiver is closed and destroyed when
> the originating document navigates away. Also track the bound Document
> in Blink's SharedWorkerClientHolder so connector pipes reconnect when
> LocalDOMWindow is reused across same-origin initial empty document
> navigations.
>
> Bug: 571966729

webui: Use widget coordinates in WebUIBubbleDialogView

ModalDialogHost::GetDialogPosition() must return coordinates relative
to GetHostView() (the host Widget). Convert the dialog position from
WebUIBubbleDialogView coordinates to widget coordinates via
ConvertPointToWidget(), and return size() from GetMaximumDialogSize().

Initialize SharedWorker connect event data attribute to the empty string

Dispatch the SharedWorker connect event with the data attribute set to
the empty string, matching the spec [1].

The change is guarded by the SharedWorkerConnectEventEmptyStringData
runtime enabled feature as a kill switch.

[1] https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/workers.html#run-a-worker

PSA: https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/blink-dev/c/kKQHWl4komE

Bug: 583449815
Low-Coverage-Reason: OTHER Disabled side of a kill switch, which keeps the previous behavior unchanged.

e7ba8c7f21d68c5b410e079088df8ce325bb2c92 [ diff ] 
crostini: Report a restore that finishes despite Cancel as done

If a disk image restore finished before a requested cancellation took
effect, the notification was removed as if the restore had been
cancelled, although the Linux disk had been replaced.

Show the "done" notification in this case, as the container restore
already does. A backup that finishes despite Cancel is still treated
as cancelled, since its file gets deleted.

Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=CrostiniExportImportTest*
